The hint says &quot;use of a similar methyl donor alone is not enough&quot; - this is warning us that just because it&#x27;s a methyltransferase doesn&#x27;t mean we can determine which one. We need to look at the specific motifs.

The &quot;GNQFCNL&quot; motif (or its variant &quot;GNTFNQFCNL&quot;) is the hallmark of PRMTs that recognize arginine residues. This motif forms the binding pocket for the arginine guanidinium group.
